M11 · CodeWhale · cfc2f2b13c07

Command execution: from /goal to receipt

How is a built-in command parsed, routed, executed, and written back to the session?

Chapter 11 / 118 source findingsmain
ROUTEQuestion→Analogy→Evidence board→Four questions→Source map→Map→Line reading
Start with a concrete analogy

A command is an airport check-in record: identify it, enforce the gate, hand it to the right counter, and write boarding and arrival receipts back to the session.

Answer the chapter question first

How is a built-in command parsed, routed, executed, and written back to the session? Read the implementation as input, state, tool effect, receipt, and recovery—not as a feature list.

Evidence board: 8 findings

Do not treat one code block as the whole system. Each card separates fact, plain language, engineering impact, caveat, and a clickable pinned source.

01
L1factarchitecture

01 · Core 把 UI 与 AI 交互拆成事件驱动的控制面

SOURCE FACT

core/mod.rs 明确把 engine、events、ops、session、tool_parser、turn 分成独立模块，并在 TUI alt-screen 中禁止直接 print_stdout/print_stderr，要求通过 tracing 和事件通道输出。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

终端画面只是一个事件消费者；真正决定模型、工具和会话怎么走的是 Core Engine。这样换成 Web、ACP 或测试宿主时，不必再复制一套 Agent Loop。

IMPLICATION

自研时应先定义 operation/event 契约，再让 TUI、HTTP 和自动化入口共享同一运行时；禁止 UI 层私自执行工具或改会话。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 1–7
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 8–15
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

Narrative from historical evidence ledger · b63e48331b7d · open ledger commit ↗
crates/tui/src/core/mod.rs:1–15PINNED ↗
    1  //! Core engine module for `DeepSeek` CLI.
    2  //!
    3  //! This module provides the event-driven architecture that separates
    4  //! the UI from the AI interaction logic:
    5  //!
    6  //! - `engine`: The main engine that processes operations
    7  //! - `events`: Events emitted by the engine to the UI
    8  //! - `ops`: Operations submitted by the UI to the engine
    9  //! - `session`: Session state management
   10  //! - `turn`: Turn context and tracking
   11
   12  // Engine code runs inside the TUI alt-screen — see `runtime_log` for why
   13  // raw stdio prints must not appear here. Use `tracing::*` instead.
   14  #![deny(clippy::print_stdout)]
   15  #![deny(clippy::print_stderr)]
   16
   17  pub mod authority;
   18  pub mod engine;
   19  pub mod events;
   20  // The first production consumer of the staged runtime contract is the
   21  // provider-neutral model boundary. Keep the remaining contract files staged
   22  // until their own consumers land instead of compiling dead scaffolding.
   23  #[path = "runtime_contract/model.rs"]
   24  pub mod model_client;
   25  pub mod ops;
   26  #[path = "runtime_contract/termination.rs"]
   27  pub mod termination;
   28  // The rest of `runtime_contract/` stays on disk as staged Core-runtime
   29  // scaffolding and remains deliberately uncompiled until it has production
   30  // consumers (TUI-DOG-017).
   31  pub mod session;
   32  pub mod tool_parser;
   33  pub mod turn;
   34
   35  // Re-exports
   36

crates/tui/src/core/mod.rs · cfc2f2b13c07CURRENT ↗
    1  //! Core engine module for `DeepSeek` CLI.
    2  //!
    3  //! This module provides the event-driven architecture that separates
    4  //! the UI from the AI interaction logic:
    5  //!
    6  //! - `engine`: The main engine that processes operations
    7  //! - `events`: Events emitted by the engine to the UI
    8  //! - `ops`: Operations submitted by the UI to the engine
    9  //! - `session`: Session state management
   10  //! - `turn`: Turn context and tracking
   11
   12  // Engine code runs inside the TUI alt-screen — see `runtime_log` for why
   13  // raw stdio prints must not appear here. Use `tracing::*` instead.
   14  #![deny(clippy::print_stdout)]
   15  #![deny(clippy::print_stderr)]
   16
   17  pub mod authority;
   18  // `crates/core` now owns the engine (issue #5261). The TUI's `engine`
   19  // consumes the core boundary directly so headless and TUI share one
   20  // `Op`-in / `EventMsg`-out API, one `ThreadId`/`SessionId` type, and one
   21  // `Journal` shape. New code should import from `codewhale_core`.
   22  pub mod engine;
   23  pub(crate) use engine::tool_catalog::allowlist_is_native_file_and_shell_only;
   24  pub mod events;
   25  // The first production consumer of the staged runtime contract is the
   26  // provider-neutral model boundary. Keep the remaining contract files staged
   27  // until their own consumers land instead of compiling dead scaffolding.
   28  #[path = "runtime_contract/model.rs"]
   29  pub mod model_client;
   30  pub mod ops;
   31  #[path = "runtime_contract/termination.rs"]
   32  pub mod termination;
   33  // The rest of `runtime_contract/` stays on disk as staged Core-runtime
   34  // scaffolding and remains deliberately uncompiled until it has production
   35  // consumers (TUI-DOG-017).
   36  pub mod session;
   37  pub mod tool_parser;
   38  pub mod turn;
   39
   40  // Re-exports
   41
02
L1factarchitecture

02 · EngineConfig 是把能力、预算和权限拧在一起的运行时总闸

SOURCE FACT

EngineConfig 同时保存 workspace、allow_shell、trust_mode、MCP/Skills/instructions、max_steps、subagent concurrency/depth/token budget、compaction、snapshots、memory、allowed/disallowed tools、max_tool_calls、hooks、strict tool mode、network policy 与 sandbox 偏好。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

CodeWhale 不是把安全、长任务和扩展散落在命令行参数里，而是先形成一份“本次会话的有效配置”，后面的 turn、工具和子 Agent 都从这份配置派生。

IMPLICATION

自研要区分静态配置、turn authority 和工具调用 authority；不要让每个工具重新解释一遍全局配置。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 221–258
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 259–298
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

Narrative from historical evidence ledger · b63e48331b7d · open ledger commit ↗
crates/tui/src/core/engine.rs:221–298PINNED ↗
  221  /// Configuration for the engine
  222  #[derive(Debug, Clone)]
  223  pub struct EngineConfig {
  224      /// Model identifier to use for responses.
  225      pub model: String,
  226      /// Route/offering limits for the active provider+model, when the runtime
  227      /// route resolver had concrete catalog facts.
  228      pub active_route_limits: Option<codewhale_config::route::RouteLimits>,
  229      /// Workspace root for tool execution and file operations.
  230      pub workspace: PathBuf,
  231      /// Allow shell tool execution when true.
  232      pub allow_shell: bool,
  233      /// Enable trust mode (skip approvals) when true.
  234      pub trust_mode: bool,
  235      /// Path to the notes file used by the notes tool.
  236      pub notes_path: PathBuf,
  237      /// Path to the MCP configuration file.
  238      pub mcp_config_path: PathBuf,
  239      /// Directory containing discoverable skills.
  240      pub skills_dir: PathBuf,
  241      /// Restrict skill discovery to CodeWhale-owned roots plus explicit
  242      /// `skills_dir` configuration.
  243      pub skills_scan_codewhale_only: bool,
  244      /// Immutable plugin authority snapshot scoped to `workspace`. Normal App
  245      /// hosts provide this explicitly; headless/embed callers that leave it
  246      /// unset receive a fresh workspace-specific snapshot in [`Engine::new`].
  247      pub plugin_registry: Option<Arc<crate::plugins::PluginRegistry>>,
  248      /// Sources injected as `<instructions source="…">` blocks in the system
  249      /// prompt (#454). Each entry is either a disk path (read at render time)
  250      /// or an inline string. Loaded in declared order from the user's
  251      /// `instructions = [...]` config or constructed by embedders.
  252      ///
  253      /// Generalized from `Vec<PathBuf>` so embedders can inject inline content
  254      /// without staging a disk file. `From<PathBuf>` impl keeps existing callers
  255      /// working with `.into()` at the call site.
  256      pub instructions: Vec<crate::prompts::InstructionSource>,
  257      pub project_context_pack_enabled: bool,
  258      /// When true, the model is instructed to respond in the current locale
  259      /// and a post-hoc translation layer replaces remaining English output.
  260      pub translation_enabled: bool,
  261      pub verbosity: Option<String>,
  262      /// Maximum number of assistant steps before stopping.
  263      pub max_steps: u32,
  264      /// Maximum number of concurrently active subagents.
  265      pub max_subagents: usize,
  266      /// Maximum queued + running sub-agents admitted for this engine session.
  267      pub max_admitted_subagents: usize,
  268      /// Number of direct (depth-1) sub-agents that may execute concurrently
  269      /// before further launches queue for a launch slot (#3095).
  270      /// Resolved from `[subagents] launch_concurrency`.
  271      pub launch_concurrency: usize,
  272      /// Whether the model-facing `agent` tool is available after applying
  273      /// feature flags and `[subagents]` opt-out controls.
  274      pub subagents_enabled: bool,
  275      /// Feature flags controlling tool availability.
  276      pub features: Features,
  277      /// Deterministic auto-review policy for tool calls.
  278      pub auto_review_policy: crate::tui::auto_review::AutoReviewPolicy,
  279      /// Auto-compaction settings for long conversations.
  280      pub compaction: CompactionConfig,
  281      /// Shared Todo list state.
  282      pub todos: SharedTodoList,
  283      /// Shared Plan state.
  284      pub plan_state: SharedPlanState,
  285      /// Shared runtime goal state for model-visible goal tools.
  286      pub goal_state: SharedGoalState,
  287      /// Maximum sub-agent recursion depth (default 3). See
  288      /// `SubAgentRuntime::max_spawn_depth`. Override via
  289      /// `[subagents] max_depth = N` in `~/.codewhale/config.toml`.
  290      pub max_spawn_depth: u32,
  291      /// Optional aggregate token budget for each root sub-agent run.
  292      /// Descendant agents inherit the root pool unless a child starts a new
  293      /// budget scope with an explicit per-call override.
  294      pub subagent_token_budget: Option<u64>,
  295      /// Per-domain network policy decider (#135). Shared across the session so
  296      /// session-scoped approvals (`/network allow <host>`) persist for the
  297      /// remainder of the run.
  298      pub network_policy: Option<crate::network_policy::NetworkPolicyDecider>,
  299      /// Whether to take side-git workspace snapshots before/after each turn.
  300      pub snapshots_enabled: bool,
  301      /// Maximum workspace size (in bytes) before snapshots self-disable on
  302      /// first init. `0` disables the cap. Resolved from
  303      /// `[snapshots] max_workspace_gb` × 1 GB at engine construction.
  304      pub snapshots_max_workspace_bytes: u64,
  305      /// Post-edit LSP diagnostics injection (#136). When `None`, the engine
  306      /// constructs a disabled manager so the field is always present.
  307      pub lsp_config: Option<crate::lsp::LspConfig>,
  308      /// Durable runtime services exposed to model-visible tools.
  309      pub runtime_services: RuntimeToolServices,
  310      /// Per-role/type sub-agent model overrides already resolved from config.
  311      pub subagent_model_overrides: HashMap<String, String>,
  312      /// Merged fleet roster (built-ins + config + personal/workspace agent
  313      /// files) shared by model-spawned sub-agents and fleet dispatch
  314      /// (#fleet-roster cutover (v0.8.67)). Defaults to built-ins only; the
  315      /// engine-config construction sites load it at session start and the setup
  316      /// wizard refreshes it after each successful profile save.
  317      pub fleet_roster: std::sync::Arc<crate::fleet::roster::FleetRoster>,
  318      /// Whether the user-memory feature is enabled (#489). When `true` the
  319      /// engine reads `memory_path` on each prompt assembly and prepends a
  320      /// `<user_memory>` block to the system prompt.
  321      pub memory_enabled: bool,
  322      /// When `true`, the legacy `memory.rs` push/inject path is deprecated
  323      /// in favour of Moraine MCP recall. `compose_block` returns `None`
  324      /// regardless of `memory_enabled`, the `remember` tool is not
  325      /// registered, and `# foo` quick-add falls through.
  326      pub moraine_fallback: bool,
  327      /// Path to the user memory file (#489). Always populated; only
  328      /// consulted when `memory_enabled` is `true`.
  329      pub memory_path: PathBuf,
  330      /// Default directory for Xiaomi MiMo speech/TTS tool outputs.
  331      pub speech_output_dir: Option<PathBuf>,
  332      pub vision_config: Option<crate::config::VisionModelConfig>,
  333      pub goal_objective: Option<String>,
  334      pub goal_token_budget: Option<u32>,
  335      pub goal_status: GoalStatus,
  336      /// Tool restriction from custom slash command frontmatter.
  337      /// `None` means the current turn may use the normal tool set.
  338      pub allowed_tools: Option<Vec<String>>,
  339      /// Tool deny-list.  Deny always wins over allow (#3027).
  340      /// `None` means no tools are explicitly denied.
  341      pub disallowed_tools: Option<Vec<String>>,
  342      /// Hard per-turn cap on admitted tool calls (#4415). `None` (the default)
  343      /// means unlimited and leaves the turn admission gate inert. Task hosts
  344      /// set this from the task's structured `max_tool_calls` constraint; the
  345      /// per-turn counter itself lives in the turn loop, not here.
  346      pub max_tool_calls: Option<u32>,
  347      /// Hook executor for control-plane hooks.
  348      /// `ToolCallBefore` hooks may deny a tool call with exit code 2.
  349      pub hook_executor: Option<std::sync::Arc<crate::hooks::HookExecutor>>,
  350      /// Resolved BCP-47 locale tag (e.g. `"en"`, `"zh-Hans"`, `"ja"`)
  351      /// for the `## Environment` block in the system prompt. The
  352      /// caller resolves this from `Settings` once at engine
  353      /// construction; the engine never touches disk for it.
  354      pub locale_tag: String,
  355      /// When true, force `tool_choice: "required"` and opt compatible function
  356      /// schemas into DeepSeek beta strict mode.
  357      pub strict_tool_mode: bool,
  358      /// Workshop / large-tool-output routing (#548). `None` disables routing.
  359      pub workshop: Option<crate::tools::large_output_router::WorkshopConfig>,
  360      /// Which search backend `web_search` should use. Default: DuckDuckGo.
  361      pub search_provider: crate::config::SearchProvider,
  362      /// API key for Tavily, Bocha, Metaso, Baidu, Volcengine, or Sofya.
  363      /// `None` for Bing, DuckDuckGo, or SearXNG.
  364      /// Metaso also falls back to the `METASO_API_KEY` env var.
  365      /// Baidu also falls back to `BAIDU_SEARCH_API_KEY`.
  366      pub search_api_key: Option<String>,
  367      /// Optional DuckDuckGo-compatible HTML endpoint override.
  368      pub search_base_url: Option<String>,
  369      /// Per-step DeepSeek API timeout for sub-agent `create_message` requests.

  221  #[derive(Debug, Clone)]
  222  pub struct EngineConfig {
  223      /// Model identifier to use for responses.
  224      pub model: String,
  225      /// Route/offering limits for the active provider+model, when the runtime
  226      /// route resolver had concrete catalog facts.
  227      pub active_route_limits: Option<codewhale_config::route::RouteLimits>,
  228      /// Workspace root for tool execution and file operations.
  229      pub workspace: PathBuf,
  230      /// Optional host-owned root for delegated-agent runtime state.
  231      ///
  232      /// When unset, the worker ledger, complete transcript artifacts and
  233      /// coordination lock retain their historical location under
  234      /// `workspace/.codewhale/state`. Embedders may set a session-scoped root
  235      /// to separate that control-plane state from the execution workspace.
  236      /// Child cwd and file authority still derive from `workspace`; hosts using
  237      /// distinct state roots for the same workspace must coordinate conflicting
  238      /// writes themselves or isolate writers with worktrees.
  239      pub subagent_state_root: Option<PathBuf>,
  240      /// Allow shell tool execution when true.
  241      pub allow_shell: bool,
  242      /// Enable trust mode (skip approvals) when true.
  243      pub trust_mode: bool,

03
L1factarchitecture

03 · Turn 运行前冻结事实，运行后再做持久化与继续决策

SOURCE FACT

Engine::run 在每个 operation 前应用待处理 runtime authority；SendMessage 路径先解析路由、输入策略、tool surface 和快照，再进入 turn loop；异常会被 catch_unwind 转成失败 TurnComplete，成功完成才触发 goal continuation，post-turn snapshot 在完成事件后异步执行。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

它先把“这次请求到底用哪个模型、哪些工具、什么权限”钉住，再让模型开跑；失败不会把会话炸掉，也不会误判成完成。

IMPLICATION

长任务系统需要 pre-dispatch receipt、失败终态和 post-turn checkpoint 三个明确边界，不能只靠一个 finally。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 1920–1930
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 1931–1942
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

04
L1factarchitecture

04 · Tool catalog 与 preview 共享同一装配函数，但 preview 是纯观察

SOURCE FACT

build_turn_tool_registry_and_catalog 被标为下一请求工具注册表和目录的唯一权威；live 模式允许连接 MCP、创建 mailbox、fork context 和 subagent runtime，preview 模式使用 Inert/PassiveSnapshot，不连接、不启动、不写 engine state，却输出同一 catalog 投影。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

用户点“查看这次会发给模型什么工具”时，不会因为预览动作偷偷启动 MCP 或子 Agent；预览和真实请求走同一个拼装逻辑，减少两套实现漂移。

IMPLICATION

Harness 应提供 side-effect-free request manifest，既服务调试也能做审批前可解释性。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 3385–3400
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 3401–3418
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

 3385      /// Build the turn's tool registry and the model-facing tool catalog.
 3386      ///
 3387      /// This is the single authority for "what tools would the next request
 3388      /// carry". `handle_send_message` calls it with [`SubAgentWiring::Live`]
 3389      /// and [`McpAccess::Connect`]; `/preview-request` calls it with
 3390      /// [`SubAgentWiring::Inert`] and [`McpAccess::PassiveSnapshot`], which
 3391      /// together remove every side effect of the build — no fork snapshot, no
 3392      /// spawned mailbox drainer, no pool creation, no `connect_all`, no status
 3393      /// events — while producing a byte-identical catalog for the state that
 3394      /// is already live.
 3395      ///
 3396      /// The session's `last_tool_catalog` is never an acceptable substitute:
 3397      /// it is one turn stale and stores the pre-activation catalog rather than
 3398      /// the active subset the provider would actually receive.
 3399      ///
 3400      /// `allowed_tools` is the command-scoped allow-list gate the catalog is
 3401      /// filtered under. It is an explicit **parameter**, not a read of
 3402      /// `self.config.allowed_tools`, because the preview's gate belongs to a
 3403      /// turn that has not been installed: writing it onto the engine and
 3404      /// restoring it afterwards would leave the wrong gate installed across
 3405      /// every `.await` in this function, and would leave it installed
 3406      /// permanently if the task were cancelled or panicked between the two
 3407      /// writes.

 3423          let todo_list = self.config.todos.clone();

 3432              let _ = self.ensure_mcp_pool().await;

 3476              let tx_event_clone = self.tx_event.clone();

L1facttools

05 · ToolSpec 把能力、审批、只读、并行和资源声明放到同一输入特化接口

SOURCE FACT

ToolSpec 要求 name/description/schema/capabilities，默认按 ExecutesCode/WritesFiles/其他能力映射 Required/Suggest/Auto；每个具体 input 还能决定 read-only、approval、parallel、detached 和 resource claims，prepare 生成 PreparedToolCall。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

工具不是只有一个名字和一个函数；系统会问“这一次具体参数是否只读、能否并行、需要什么审批、会占什么资源”。

IMPLICATION

工具能力要来自类型和实际 input，而不是维护一份会漏掉插件/MCP 的名字黑名单。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 1158–1186
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 1187–1217
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

 1175          let caps = self.capabilities();
 1176          if caps.contains(&ToolCapability::ExecutesCode) {
 1177              ApprovalRequirement::Required
 1178          } else if caps.contains(&ToolCapability::WritesFiles) {
 1179              ApprovalRequirement::Suggest
 1180          } else {

 1182          }
 1183      }

 1194      }

 1198          let caps = self.capabilities();

 1200              && !caps.contains(&ToolCapability::WritesFiles)
 1201              && !caps.contains(&ToolCapability::ExecutesCode)
 1202      }

 1227      ///
 1228      /// Resource claims deliberately default to global exclusivity until a
 1229      /// first-party tool opts into narrower, canonicalized claims. The initial
 1230      /// seam records this decision but leaves the existing scheduler unchanged.
 1231      fn prepare(&self, input: Value, _context: &ToolContext) -> Result<PreparedToolCall, ToolError> {

 1236              supports_parallel: self.supports_parallel_for(&input),
 1237              starts_detached: self.starts_detached_for(&input),
 1238              approval: self.approval_requirement_for(&input),

 1250  /// Gather LSP diagnostics for `paths` using the manager stored in `context`,
 1251  /// and return the rendered `<diagnostics …>` blocks joined by newlines.
 1252  ///
 1253  /// Returns an empty string when:
 1254  /// - `context.lsp_manager` is `None`
 1255  /// - the manager's `enabled` flag is `false`
 1256  /// - none of the files produce diagnostics (e.g. all clean, or language unknown)
 1257  ///
 1258  /// This function is non-blocking by design: every failure mode (missing LSP
 1259  /// binary, timeout, unknown language) degrades to an empty string rather than
 1260  /// propagating an error to the caller.
 1261  pub async fn lsp_diagnostics_for_paths(context: &ToolContext, paths: &[PathBuf]) -> String {
 1262      use crate::lsp::render_blocks;
 1263
 1264      let manager = match context.lsp_manager.as_ref() {
 1265          Some(m) if m.config().enabled => m,
 1266          _ => return String::new(),
 1267      };
 1268
 1269      let mut blocks = Vec::new();
 1270      for (idx, path) in paths.iter().enumerate() {
 1271          if let Some(block) = manager.diagnostics_for(path, idx as u64).await {
 1272              blocks.push(block);
 1273          }
 1274      }
 1275
 1276      render_blocks(&blocks)
 1277  }

 1287                  prefix = Some(prefix_component.as_os_str().to_owned());

L1facttools

06 · Registry 执行前会重新施加 machine authority，并提供只读事实投影

SOURCE FACT

ToolRegistry execute_full 在真正执行前调用 enforce_tool_authority；registry_facts 只导出名称、描述、可见性、能力、审批和插件标记，不暴露可执行对象；名称解析有大小写、空格/连字符、CamelCase、后缀和模糊匹配的确定性阶梯。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

预览层拿到的是一份不能执行的菜单，执行层还要再验一次；模型叫错 `read-file` 也会按固定规则解析，不会随机挑工具。

IMPLICATION

把 facts projection 和 executable registry 分离，是构建 request inspector、审计 UI 和安全边界的关键。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 91–94
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 95–99
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

  101      /// Execute a tool with an optional context override.
  102      ///
  103      /// This is used for retrying tools with elevated sandbox policies.
  104      /// After execution, results are stamped with adaptive evidence routing.
  105      pub async fn execute_full_with_context(
  106          &self,
  107          name: &str,
  108          input: Value,
  109          context_override: Option<&ToolContext>,
  110      ) -> Result<ToolResult, ToolError> {
  111          let tool = self
  112              .get(name)
  113              .ok_or_else(|| ToolError::not_available(format!("tool '{name}' is not registered")))?;
  114
  115          let ctx = context_override.unwrap_or(&self.context);
  116          enforce_tool_authority(name, &input, tool.as_ref(), ctx)?;
  117          let mut result = tool.execute(input.clone(), ctx).await?;
  118
  119          // Adaptive evidence routing (#4619) is storage-free here because this
  120          // layer does not own a call id. The engine/subagent completion boundary
  121          // publishes the exact artifact. Classic workshop previews remain an
  122          // explicit local rollback path.
  123          let raw_bypass = input.get("raw").and_then(|v| v.as_bool()).unwrap_or(false);
  124
  125          if let Some(router) = ctx.large_output_router.as_ref() {
  126              use crate::tools::large_output_router::{
  127                  LargeOutputRouter, RouteDecision, classic_output_routing_enabled,
  128              };
  129              if !classic_output_routing_enabled() {
  130                  let (routing, estimated_tokens, threshold) =
  131                      router.evidence_routing(name, &result, raw_bypass);
  132                  let metadata = result.metadata.get_or_insert_with(|| serde_json::json!({}));
  133                  if let Some(object) = metadata.as_object_mut() {
  134                      object.insert(
  135                          "evidence_routing".to_string(),
  136                          serde_json::to_value(routing)
  137                              .unwrap_or_else(|_| serde_json::json!("inline")),
  138                      );
  139                      object.insert(
  140                          "evidence_estimated_tokens".to_string(),
  141                          estimated_tokens.into(),
  142                      );
  143                      object.insert("evidence_threshold_tokens".to_string(), threshold.into());
  144                  }
  145                  return Ok(result);
  146              }
  147              match router.route(name, &result, raw_bypass) {
  148                  RouteDecision::PassThrough => {}
  149                  RouteDecision::Synthesise {
  150                      estimated_tokens,
  151                      threshold,
  152                  } => {
  153                      // Store the raw output in the workshop variable store.
  154                      if let Some(vars_arc) = ctx.workshop_vars.as_ref() {
  155                          let mut vars = vars_arc.lock().await;
  156                          vars.store_raw(name, &result.content);
  157                      }
  158
  159                      // Build a terse synthesis using the same model the registry
  160                      // was constructed for (workshop Flash model). For now we
  161                      // produce a structured header + truncated preview without
  162                      // a live API call so the engine stays dependency-free at
  163                      // the registry layer. A follow-up can wire in the Flash
  164                      // client when the async LLM call is safe here.
  165                      let preview_chars = 1_200usize;
  166                      let preview: String = result.content.chars().take(preview_chars).collect();
  167                      let ellipsis = if result.content.chars().count() > preview_chars {
  168                          "\n… [output truncated — full text in workshop variable `last_tool_result`]"
  169                      } else {
  170                          ""

  117      /// After execution, results are stamped with adaptive evidence routing.
  118      #[allow(dead_code)] // compatibility seam for text-only internal callers
  119      pub async fn execute_full_with_context(
  120          &self,
  121          name: &str,
  122          input: Value,
  123          context_override: Option<&ToolContext>,
  124      ) -> Result<ToolResult, ToolError> {
  125          self.execute_rich_full_with_context(name, input, context_override)
  126              .await
  127              .map(RichToolResult::into_result)
  128      }
  129
  130      pub(crate) async fn execute_rich_full_with_context(
  131          &self,
  132          name: &str,
  133          input: Value,
  134          context_override: Option<&ToolContext>,
  135      ) -> Result<RichToolResult, ToolError> {
  136          let tool = self
  137              .get(name)
  138              .ok_or_else(|| ToolError::not_available(format!("tool '{name}' is not registered")))?;
  139
  140          let ctx = context_override.unwrap_or(&self.context);
  141          enforce_tool_authority(name, &input, tool.as_ref(), ctx)?;
  142          let mut rich = crate::image_attach::bound_rich_tool_result(
  143              tool.execute_rich(input.clone(), ctx).await?,
  144          );
  145          let result = &mut rich.result;
  146
  147          // Adaptive evidence routing (#4619) is storage-free here because this
  148          // layer does not own a call id. The engine/subagent completion boundary
  149          // publishes the exact artifact. Classic workshop previews remain an
  150          // explicit local rollback path.
  151          let raw_bypass = input.get("raw").and_then(|v| v.as_bool()).unwrap_or(false);
  152
  153          if let Some(router) = ctx.large_output_router.as_ref() {
  154              use crate::tools::large_output_router::{
  155                  EvidenceRouting, LargeOutputRouter, RouteDecision, classic_output_routing_enabled,
  156              };
  157              if !classic_output_routing_enabled() {
  158                  let (estimated_routing, estimated_tokens, threshold) =
  159                      router.evidence_routing(name, result, raw_bypass);
  160                  let metadata = result.metadata.get_or_insert_with(|| serde_json::json!({}));
  161                  if let Some(object) = metadata.as_object_mut() {
  162                      // A tool that self-bounds its output behind its own
  163                      // recovery contract (e.g. read_file's `next_start_line`
  164                      // paging) declares its routing itself; the size estimate
  165                      // must not override that and double-wrap the result.
  166                      let routing = object
  167                          .get("evidence_routing")
  168                          .cloned()
  169                          .and_then(|value| serde_json::from_value::<EvidenceRouting>(value).ok())
  170                          .unwrap_or(estimated_routing);
07
L1facttools

07 · 并行工具只允许 read-only、Auto approval 且声明 supports_parallel

SOURCE FACT

execute_parallel_tool 对每个调用检查 MCP parallel-safe 白名单，普通工具必须 registered、input-specific read-only、approval=Auto、supports_parallel=true；通过后用 FuturesUnordered 并发，shell 额外受 Semaphore 限流。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

并行不是模型说了算：写文件、需要询问用户或没声明线程安全的工具都不能塞进并行批次。

IMPLICATION

并发调度要让工具自己声明安全性，再由 scheduler 做交叉检查；不能只按工具名字分组。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 230–257
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 258–287
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

crates/tui/src/core/engine/tool_execution.rs:230–287PINNED ↗
  230      pub(super) async fn execute_parallel_tool(
  231          &mut self,
  232          input: serde_json::Value,
  233          tool_registry: Option<&crate::tools::ToolRegistry>,
  234          tool_exec_lock: Arc<RwLock<()>>,
  235          context_override: Option<crate::tools::ToolContext>,
  236      ) -> Result<ToolResult, ToolError> {
  237          let calls = parse_parallel_tool_calls(&input)?;
  238          let mcp_pool = if calls.iter().any(|(tool, _)| McpPool::is_mcp_tool(tool)) {
  239              Some(self.ensure_mcp_pool().await?)
  240          } else {
  241              None
  242          };
  243          let Some(registry) = tool_registry else {
  244              return Err(ToolError::not_available(
  245                  "tool registry unavailable for multi_tool_use.parallel",
  246              ));
  247          };
  248
  249          let result_count = calls.len();
  250          let mut tasks = FuturesUnordered::new();
  251          let shell_permits = Arc::new(tokio::sync::Semaphore::new(MAX_PARALLEL_SHELL_EXEC));
  252          for (index, (tool_name, tool_input)) in calls.into_iter().enumerate() {
  253              if tool_name == MULTI_TOOL_PARALLEL_NAME {
  254                  return Err(ToolError::invalid_input(
  255                      "multi_tool_use.parallel cannot call itself",
  256                  ));
  257              }
  258              if McpPool::is_mcp_tool(&tool_name) {
  259                  if !mcp_tool_is_parallel_safe(&tool_name) {
  260                      return Err(ToolError::invalid_input(format!(
  261                          "Tool '{tool_name}' is an MCP tool and cannot run in parallel. \
  262                           Allowed MCP tools: list_mcp_resources, list_mcp_resource_templates, \
  263                           mcp_read_resource, read_mcp_resource, mcp_get_prompt."
  264                      )));
  265                  }
  266              } else {
  267                  let Some(spec) = registry.get(&tool_name) else {
  268                      return Err(ToolError::not_available(format!(
  269                          "tool '{tool_name}' is not registered"
  270                      )));
  271                  };
  272                  if !spec.is_read_only_for(&tool_input) {
  273                      return Err(ToolError::invalid_input(format!(
  274                          "Tool '{tool_name}' is not read-only and cannot run in parallel"
  275                      )));
  276                  }
  277                  if spec.approval_requirement_for(&tool_input) != ApprovalRequirement::Auto {
  278                      return Err(ToolError::invalid_input(format!(
  279                          "Tool '{tool_name}' requires approval and cannot run in parallel"
  280                      )));
  281                  }
  282                  if !spec.supports_parallel_for(&tool_input) {
  283                      return Err(ToolError::invalid_input(format!(
  284                          "Tool '{tool_name}' does not support parallel execution"
  285                      )));
  286                  }
  287              }
  288
  289              let registry_ref = registry;
  290              let lock = tool_exec_lock.clone();
  291              let tx_event = self.tx_event.clone();
  292              let mcp_pool = mcp_pool.clone();
  293              let shell_permits = shell_permits.clone();
  294              let workspace = self.session.workspace.clone();
  295              let context_override = context_override.clone();
  296              tasks.push(async move {
  297                  let _shell_permit = if tool_name == "exec_shell" {
  298                      shell_permits.acquire_owned().await.ok()
  299                  } else {
  300                      None
  301                  };
  302                  let result = Engine::execute_tool_with_lock(
  303                      lock,
  304                      true,
  305                      false,
  306                      tx_event,
  307                      tool_name.clone(),
  308                      tool_input.clone(),
  309                      workspace,
  310                      Some(registry_ref),
  311                      mcp_pool,
  312                      context_override,
  313                  )
  314                  .await;
  315                  (index, tool_name, result)
  316              });
  317          }
  318
  319          let mut results: Vec<Option<ParallelToolResultEntry>> = Vec::with_capacity(result_count);
  320          results.resize_with(result_count, || None);
  321          while let Some((index, tool_name, result)) = tasks.next().await {
  322              let entry = match result {
  323                  Ok(output) => {
  324                      let mut error = None;
  325                      if !output.success {
  326                          error = Some(output.content.clone());
  327                      }
  328                      ParallelToolResultEntry {
  329                          tool_name,
  330                          success: output.success,
  331                          content: output.content,
  332                          error,
  333                      }
  334                  }
  335                  Err(err) => {
  336                      let message = format!("{err}");
  337                      ParallelToolResultEntry {
  338                          tool_name,
  339                          success: false,
  340                          content: format!("Error: {message}"),
  341                          error: Some(message),
  342                      }
  343                  }
  344              };
  345              results[index] = Some(entry);
  346          }
  347          let results = results.into_iter().flatten().collect();
  348
  349          ToolResult::json(&ParallelToolResult { results })
  350              .map_err(|e| ToolError::execution_failed(e.to_string()))
  351      }

  355          lock: Arc<RwLock<()>>,
  356          supports_parallel: bool,

  358          tx_event: mpsc::Sender<Event>,

08
L1facttools

08 · 工具执行有 heartbeat、读写锁、交互终端 RAII 和结构化结束日志

SOURCE FACT

execute_tool_with_lock 在抢锁前启动 ToolHeartbeatGuard，read-only/parallel 走读锁、其他工具走写锁；InteractiveTerminalGuard 在取消时也保证 ResumeEvents，结束日志记录 dispatch、耗时、输出大小和 typed error kind。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

一个长时间 build 不会被 UI 误判为死掉；并发读不会阻塞，写会排他；交互终端中途取消也会恢复 TUI 状态。

IMPLICATION

可观测性要覆盖等待资源的时间，而不只是工具函数真正开始后的耗时。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 353–378
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 379–406
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

Narrative from historical evidence ledger · b63e48331b7d · open ledger commit ↗
crates/tui/src/core/engine/tool_execution.rs:353–406PINNED ↗
  353      #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
  354      pub(super) async fn execute_tool_with_lock(
  355          lock: Arc<RwLock<()>>,
  356          supports_parallel: bool,
  357          interactive: bool,
  358          tx_event: mpsc::Sender<Event>,
  359          tool_name: String,
  360          tool_input: serde_json::Value,
  361          workspace: PathBuf,
  362          registry: Option<&crate::tools::ToolRegistry>,
  363          mcp_pool: Option<Arc<AsyncMutex<McpPool>>>,
  364          context_override: Option<crate::tools::ToolContext>,
  365      ) -> Result<ToolResult, ToolError> {
  366          // This guard starts before lock acquisition, so contention as well as
  367          // registry/MCP/interpreter execution remains visibly live.
  368          let _heartbeat = ToolHeartbeatGuard::start(tx_event.clone(), TOOL_HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL);
  369          let started_at = std::time::Instant::now();
  370          let dispatch = if McpPool::is_mcp_tool(&tool_name) {
  371              "mcp"
  372          } else if matches!(
  373              tool_name.as_str(),
  374              CODE_EXECUTION_TOOL_NAME | JS_EXECUTION_TOOL_NAME
  375          ) {
  376              "interpreter"
  377          } else if registry.is_some() {
  378              "registry"
  379          } else {
  380              "missing"
  381          };
  382          let input_bytes = serde_json::to_string(&tool_input)
  383              .map(|s| s.len())
  384              .unwrap_or(0);
  385          tracing::debug!(
  386              target: "engine.tool_execution",
  387              tool = %tool_name,
  388              dispatch,
  389              interactive,
  390              supports_parallel,
  391              input_bytes,
  392              "tool.exec.start",
  393          );
  394
  395          let _guard = if supports_parallel {
  396              ToolExecGuard::Read(lock.read().await)
  397          } else {
  398              ToolExecGuard::Write(lock.write().await)
  399          };
  400
  401          // RAII pause/resume: ensures `Event::ResumeEvents` always fires on
  402          // drop, even if the tool future is cancelled mid-await. See
  403          // `InteractiveTerminalGuard` doc-comment for the regression this
  404          // closes (parent terminal scrollback hijacking the TUI after a
  405          // cancelled interactive tool).
  406          let _terminal = InteractiveTerminalGuard::engage(tx_event, interactive).await;
  407
  408          let tool_authority = context_override
  409              .as_ref()
  410              .and_then(|context| context.tool_authority.as_ref())
  411              .or_else(|| registry.and_then(|registry| registry.context().tool_authority.as_ref()));
  412          if let Some(authority) = tool_authority {
  413              if McpPool::is_mcp_tool(&tool_name)
  414                  && !super::dispatch::mcp_tool_is_read_only(&tool_name)
  415              {
  416                  return Err(ToolError::permission_denied(format!(
  417                      "worker '{}' cannot run mutating MCP tool {tool_name}: it has no authorized file target",
  418                      authority.owner
  419                  )));
  420              }
  421              if matches!(
  422                  tool_name.as_str(),
  423                  CODE_EXECUTION_TOOL_NAME | JS_EXECUTION_TOOL_NAME
  424              ) {
  425                  return Err(ToolError::permission_denied(format!(
  426                      "worker '{}' cannot run {tool_name}: arbitrary code execution is outside its machine-readable authority envelope",
  427                      authority.owner
  428                  )));
  429              }
  430          }
  431
  432          let outcome = if McpPool::is_mcp_tool(&tool_name) {
  433              if let Some(pool) = mcp_pool {
  434                  Engine::execute_mcp_tool_with_pool(pool, &tool_name, tool_input).await
  435              } else {
  436                  Err(ToolError::not_available(format!(
  437                      "tool '{tool_name}' is not registered"
  438                  )))
  439              }
  440          } else if tool_name == CODE_EXECUTION_TOOL_NAME {
  441              execute_code_execution_tool(&tool_input, &workspace).await
  442          } else if tool_name == JS_EXECUTION_TOOL_NAME {
  443              execute_js_execution_tool(&tool_input, &workspace).await
  444          } else if let Some(registry) = registry {
  445              registry
  446                  .execute_full_with_context(&tool_name, tool_input, context_override.as_ref())
  447                  .await
  448          } else {
  449              Err(ToolError::not_available(format!(
  450                  "tool '{tool_name}' is not registered"
  451              )))
  452          };
  453
  454          let duration_ms = started_at.elapsed().as_millis() as u64;
  455          match &outcome {
  456              Ok(result) => {
  457                  tracing::debug!(
  458                      target: "engine.tool_execution",
  459                      tool = %tool_name,
  460                      dispatch,
  461                      duration_ms,
  462                      success = result.success,
  463                      output_bytes = result.content.len(),
  464                      "tool.exec.end",
  465                  );
  466              }
  467              Err(err) => {
  468                  let kind = match err {
  469                      ToolError::InvalidInput { .. } => "invalid_input",
  470                      ToolError::MissingField { .. } => "missing_field",
  471                      ToolError::PathEscape { .. } => "path_escape",
  472                      ToolError::ExecutionFailed { .. } => "execution_failed",
  473                      ToolError::Timeout { .. } => "timeout",
  474                      ToolError::Cancelled { .. } => "cancelled",
  475                      ToolError::NotAvailable { .. } => "not_available",
  476                      ToolError::PermissionDenied { .. } => "permission_denied",
  477                  };

  355          }

  359              .map_err(|e| ToolError::execution_failed(e.to_string()))?;

  361              RichToolResult::with_content_blocks(result, content_blocks),
  362          ))
  363      }
  364
  365      #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
  366      pub(super) async fn execute_tool_with_lock(
  367          lock: Arc<RwLock<()>>,
  368          supports_parallel: bool,
  369          interactive: bool,
  370          tx_event: mpsc::Sender<Event>,
  371          cancel_token: Option<CancellationToken>,
  372          tool_name: String,
  373          tool_input: serde_json::Value,
  374          workspace: PathBuf,
  375          registry: Option<&crate::tools::ToolRegistry>,
  376          mcp_pool: Option<Arc<AsyncMutex<McpPool>>>,
  377          context_override: Option<crate::tools::ToolContext>,
  378      ) -> Result<RichToolResult, ToolError> {
  379          if cancel_token
  380              .as_ref()
  381              .is_some_and(CancellationToken::is_cancelled)
  382          {
  383              return Err(ToolError::permission_denied(
  384                  "Turn stopped by user. Tool call blocked.",
  385              ));
  386          }
  387          // This guard starts before lock acquisition, so contention as well as
  388          // registry/MCP/interpreter execution remains visibly live.
  389          let _heartbeat = ToolHeartbeatGuard::start(tx_event.clone(), TOOL_HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL);
  390          let started_at = std::time::Instant::now();
  391          let dispatch = if McpPool::is_mcp_tool(&tool_name) {
  392              "mcp"
  393          } else if matches!(
  394              tool_name.as_str(),
  395              CODE_EXECUTION_TOOL_NAME | JS_EXECUTION_TOOL_NAME
  396          ) {
  397              "interpreter"
  398          } else if registry.is_some() {
  399              "registry"
  400          } else {
  401              "missing"
  402          };
  403          let input_bytes = serde_json::to_string(&tool_input)
  404              .map(|s| s.len())
  405              .unwrap_or(0);
  406          tracing::debug!(
  407              target: "engine.tool_execution",
  408              tool = %tool_name,
  409              dispatch,
  410              interactive,
  411              supports_parallel,
  412              input_bytes,
  413              "tool.exec.start",
  414          );
  415
  416          let _guard = if supports_parallel {
  417              ToolExecGuard::Read(lock.read().await)
  418          } else {
  419              ToolExecGuard::Write(lock.write().await)
  420          };
  421
  422          // RAII pause/resume: ensures `Event::ResumeEvents` always fires on
  423          // drop, even if the tool future is cancelled mid-await. See
  424          // `InteractiveTerminalGuard` doc-comment for the regression this
  425          // closes (parent terminal scrollback hijacking the TUI after a
  426          // cancelled interactive tool).
  427          let _terminal = InteractiveTerminalGuard::engage(tx_event, interactive).await;
  428
  429          if cancel_token
  430              .as_ref()
  431              .is_some_and(CancellationToken::is_cancelled)
  432          {
  433              return Err(ToolError::permission_denied(
  434                  "Turn stopped by user. Tool call blocked.",
  435              ));
  436          }
  437
  438          let tool_authority = context_override
  439              .as_ref()
  440              .and_then(|context| context.tool_authority.as_ref())
  441              .or_else(|| registry.and_then(|registry| registry.context().tool_authority.as_ref()));
  442          if let Some(authority) = tool_authority {
  443              if McpPool::is_mcp_tool(&tool_name)
  444                  && !super::dispatch::mcp_tool_is_read_only(&tool_name)
  445              {
  446                  return Err(ToolError::permission_denied(format!(
  447                      "worker '{}' cannot run mutating MCP tool {tool_name}: it has no authorized file target",
  448                      authority.owner
  449                  )));
  450              }
  451              if matches!(
  452                  tool_name.as_str(),
  453                  CODE_EXECUTION_TOOL_NAME | JS_EXECUTION_TOOL_NAME
  454              ) {
  455                  return Err(ToolError::permission_denied(format!(
  456                      "worker '{}' cannot run {tool_name}: arbitrary code execution is outside its machine-readable authority envelope",
  457                      authority.owner
  458                  )));
  459              }
  460          }
  461
  462          let outcome: Result<RichToolResult, ToolError> = if McpPool::is_mcp_tool(&tool_name) {
  463              if let Some(pool) = mcp_pool {
  464                  Engine::execute_mcp_tool_with_pool(pool, &tool_name, tool_input)
  465                      .await
  466                      .map(RichToolResult::plain)
  467              } else {
  468                  Err(ToolError::not_available(format!(
  469                      "tool '{tool_name}' is not registered"
  470                  )))
  471              }
  472          } else if tool_name == CODE_EXECUTION_TOOL_NAME {
  473              execute_code_execution_tool(&tool_input, &workspace)
  474                  .await
  475                  .map(RichToolResult::plain)
  476          } else if tool_name == JS_EXECUTION_TOOL_NAME {
  477              execute_js_execution_tool(&tool_input, &workspace)

  213      fn from_value_accepts_four_options_and_flags() {
  214          // Mirrors the json!-literal style used in tools/subagent/tests.rs and
  215          // exercises the schema-loosening from issue #3102: 4 options (was capped
  216          // at 3) plus the new allow_free_text / multi_select flags.
  217          let input = json!({
  218              "questions": [{
  219                  "header": "Scope",
  220                  "id": "scope",
  221                  "question": "Which surfaces should this change affect?",
  222                  "options": [
  223                      { "label": "TUI", "description": "Visible modal flow only" },
  224                      { "label": "Headless", "description": "Protocol event only" },
  225                      { "label": "All surfaces", "description": "TUI and headless" },
  226                      { "label": "CLI", "description": "Command-line surface" }
  227                  ],
  228                  "allow_free_text": true,
  229                  "multi_select": true
  230              }]
  231          });
  232          let request = UserInputRequest::from_value(&input).expect("4 options + flags parse");
  233          assert_eq!(request.questions.len(), 1);
  234          assert_eq!(request.questions[0].options.len(), 4);
  235          assert!(request.questions[0].allow_free_text);
  236          assert!(request.questions[0].multi_select);
  237      }

 1276  fn detect_host_glibc() -> Option<GlibcVersion> {
 1277      let getconf = std::process::Command::new("getconf")
 1278          .arg("GNU_LIBC_VERSION")
 1279          .output()
 1280          .ok()
 1281          .filter(|output| output.status.success())
 1282          .and_then(|output| String::from_utf8(output.stdout).ok())
 1283          .and_then(|output| parse_glibc_version(&output));
 1284      if getconf.is_some() {
 1285          return getconf;
 1286      }
 1287
 1288      std::process::Command::new("ldd")
 1289          .arg("--version")
 1290          .output()
 1291          .ok()
 1292          .filter(|output| output.status.success())
 1293          .and_then(|output| {
 1294              let mut text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout).to_string();
 1295              if text.trim().is_empty() {
 1296                  text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr).to_string();
 1297              }
 1298              parse_glibc_version(&text)
 1299          })
 1300  }
 1301
 1302  fn glibc_compatibility_message(
 1303      asset_name: &str,
 1304      required: GlibcVersion,
 1305      host: Option<GlibcVersion>,
 1306  ) -> String {
 1307      let host_line = match host {
 1308          Some(host) => format!(
 1309              "this system has glibc {}, which is too old for that asset.",
 1310              host.display()
 1311          ),
 1312          None => "this system does not appear to provide GNU libc.".to_string(),
 1313      };
 1314      format!(
 1315          "\
 1316  Prebuilt Codewhale asset `{asset_name}` requires GLIBC_{required}, but {host_line}
 1317
 1318  Official Linux release binaries are GNU libc builds. Ubuntu 22.04 ships glibc
 1319  2.35, so it cannot run a binary that was built against Ubuntu 24.04/glibc 2.39.
 1320
 1321  Install from source on this host instead:
 1322
 1323    cargo install codewhale-cli --locked
 1324
 1325  Release engineering follow-up: build Linux GNU assets against an older glibc
 1326  baseline, or add a musl/static Linux asset. Set CODEWHALE_SKIP_GLIBC_CHECK=1 to
 1327  bypass this preflight at your own risk.",
 1328          required = required.display(),
 1329      )
 1330  }

git→parse→route→policy→handler→receipt
Expand line-level source evidence
  403
  404  /// Find the enclosing git repository root. Works for nested workspaces and
  405  /// worktrees where `.git` is a file instead of a directory.
  406  fn git_root(workspace: &Path) -> Option<PathBuf> {
  407      let direct_git_marker = workspace.join(".git");
  408      let discovered = Command::new("git")
  409          .args(["rev-parse", "--show-toplevel"])
  410          .current_dir(workspace)
  411          .output()
  412          .ok()
  413          .and_then(|out| {
  414              if out.status.success() {
  415                  String::from_utf8(out.stdout)
  416                      .ok()
  417                      .map(|s| s.trim().to_string())
  418                      .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())
  419                      .map(PathBuf::from)
  420              } else {
  421                  None
  422              }
  423          });
  424      discovered.or_else(|| direct_git_marker.exists().then(|| workspace.to_path_buf()))
  425  }
  426
  427  /// Gather git repository information via subprocess calls.
  428  fn gather_git_info(workspace: &Path) -> Option<String> {
  429      let git_root = git_root(workspace)?;
  430
  431      let run = |args: &[&str]| -> Option<String> {
  432          Command::new("git")
  433              .args(args)
  434              .current_dir(&git_root)
  435              .output()
  436              .ok()
  437              .and_then(|out| {
  438                  if out.status.success() {
  439                      String::from_utf8(out.stdout)
  440                          .ok()
  441                          .map(|s| s.trim().to_string())
  442                          .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())
  443                  } else {
  444                      None
  445                  }
  446              })
  447      };
  448
  449      let mut lines: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
  450
  451      // Remote URL (strip embedded credentials to avoid leaking tokens to the LLM).
  452      if let Some(url) = run(&["remote", "get-url", "origin"]) {
  453          let sanitized = strip_url_credentials(&url);
  454          lines.push(format!("- Remote: {sanitized}"));
  455      }
  456
  457      // Current branch.
  458      if let Some(branch) = run(&["rev-parse", "--abbrev-ref", "HEAD"]) {
  459          lines.push(format!("- Branch: {branch}"));
  460      }
  461
  462      // Status summary.
  463      let status_output = Command::new("git")
  464          .args(["status", "--porcelain=v1", "--untracked-files=no"])
  465          .current_dir(&git_root)
  466          .output()
  467          .ok();

  139  /// HTML-escape special characters.
  140  fn html_escape(s: &str) -> String {
  141      s.replace('&', "&amp;")
  142          .replace('<', "&lt;")
  143          .replace('>', "&gt;")
  144          .replace('"', "&quot;")
  145          .replace('\'', "&#39;")
  146  }
  147
  148  /// Write HTML to a secure temp file and keep it alive for upload.
  149  fn write_temp_html(html: &str) -> Result<tempfile::NamedTempFile, String> {
  150      let mut tmp = tempfile::Builder::new()
  151          .prefix("codewhale-share-")
  152          .suffix(".html")
  153          .tempfile()
  154          .map_err(|e| format!("{e}"))?;
  155      tmp.write_all(html.as_bytes()).map_err(|e| format!("{e}"))?;
  156      Ok(tmp)
  157  }

   31          &COMMAND_INFO

   33
   34      fn execute(_app: &mut App, arg: Option<&str>) -> CommandResult {
   35          match arg.map(str::trim).filter(|s| !s.is_empty()) {
   36              Some("on") | Some("enable") | Some("yes") | Some("1") | Some("true") => {
   37                  CommandResult::action(AppAction::SetAdvisorEnabled { enabled: true })
   38              }
   39              Some("off") | Some("disable") | Some("no") | Some("0") | Some("false") => {
   40                  CommandResult::action(AppAction::SetAdvisorEnabled { enabled: false })
   41              }
   42              Some("status") | None => {
   43                  // The engine owns the authoritative state; report from config.
   44                  CommandResult::message(
   45                      "Advisor status: use `/advisor on` or `/advisor off` to toggle. \
   46                       Check `[advisor] enabled` in config.toml for the session default.",
   47                  )
   48              }
   49              Some(unknown) => CommandResult::error(format!(
   50                  "Unknown advisor argument: {unknown:?}. Use `on`, `off`, or `status`."
   51              )),
   52          }
   53      }
   54  }

   16  pub(in crate::commands) struct AgentCmd;

   18  impl RegisterCommand for AgentCmd {
   19      fn info() -> &'static CommandInfo {
   20          &COMMAND_INFO

   22
   23      fn execute(app: &mut App, arg: Option<&str>) -> CommandResult {
   24          agent(app, arg)
   25      }
   26  }
   27
   28  pub fn agent(_app: &mut App, arg: Option<&str>) -> CommandResult {
   29      if let Some(action) = parse_agent_control_action(arg) {
   30          if action.action == "cancel" {
   31              return CommandResult::with_message_and_action(
   32                  format!("Cancelling agent {}...", action.agent_id),
   33                  AppAction::CancelSubAgent {
   34                      agent_id: action.agent_id,
   35                  },
   36              );
   37          }
   38          let message = format!(
   39              "Call `agent` with action `{}`, agent_id `{}`, then summarize the returned status for the user. Do not start a new agent.",
   40              action.action, action.agent_id
   41          );
   42          return CommandResult::with_message_and_action(
   43              format!("Agent {} requested for {}.", action.action, action.agent_id),
   44              AppAction::SendMessage(message),
   45          );
   46      }
   47
   48      let (max_depth, task) = match super::util::parse_depth_prefixed_arg(arg, 1) {
   49          Ok(parsed) => parsed,
   50          Err(message) => return CommandResult::error(message),
   51      };
   52      let task = match task {
   53          Some(task) if !task.trim().is_empty() => task.trim().to_string(),
   54          _ => {
   55              return CommandResult::error(
   56                  "Usage: /agent [N] <task>\n\n\
   57                   Opens a persistent sub-agent session with recursive agent depth N (0-3, default 1).",
   58              );
   59          }
   60      };
   61      let message = format!(
   62          "Launch one sub-agent for this task by calling `agent` with name `slash_agent`, `prompt: {task:?}`, and `max_depth: {max_depth}`. Use `handle_read` on the returned transcript_handle if you need more detail. Verify any claimed side effects before reporting success."
   63      );

   65          format!("Opening persistent sub-agent at depth {max_depth}..."),

   67      )

   29      fn info() -> &'static CommandInfo {
   30          &COMMAND_INFO
   31      }

   35      }

   37
   38  /// Handle the `/anchor` command with subcommands:
   39  /// - `/anchor <text>` — add a new anchor
   40  /// - `/anchor list` — list all anchors
   41  /// - `/anchor remove <n>` — remove anchor by 1-based index
   42  pub fn anchor(app: &mut App, content: Option<&str>) -> CommandResult {
   43      let input = match content {
   44          Some(c) => c.trim(),
   45          None => {
   46              return CommandResult::error(format!("Usage: {USAGE}"));
   47          }
   48      };
   49
   50      if input.is_empty() {
   51          return CommandResult::error(format!("Usage: {USAGE}"));
   52      }
   53
   54      // Parse subcommands.
   55      if input.eq_ignore_ascii_case("list") {
   56          return list_anchors(app);
   57      }
   58
   59      if let Some(rest) = input
   60          .strip_prefix("remove ")
   61          .or_else(|| input.strip_prefix("rm "))
   62          .or_else(|| input.strip_prefix("delete "))
   63      {
   64          return remove_anchor(app, rest.trim());
   65      }
   66
   67      // Default: add a new anchor.
   68      add_anchor(app, input)
   69  }
   70
   71  fn anchors_path(app: &App) -> std::path::PathBuf {
   72      let primary = app.workspace.join(".codewhale").join("anchors.md");
   73      if primary.exists() {
   74          return primary;
   75      }
   76      app.workspace.join(".deepseek").join("anchors.md")
   77  }
   78
   79  /// Read and split anchors from the file. Each anchor is separated by "\n---\n".

    6  use crate::commands::traits::{CommandInfo, RegisterCommand};

   10  pub(in crate::commands) const COMMAND_INFO: CommandInfo = CommandInfo {
   11      name: "attach",
   12      aliases: &["image", "media", "fujian"],
   13      usage: "/attach <path>",
   14      description_id: MessageId::CmdAttachDescription,
   15  };
   16
   17  pub(in crate::commands) struct AttachCmd;
   18
   19  impl RegisterCommand for AttachCmd {
   20      fn info() -> &'static CommandInfo {
   21          &COMMAND_INFO
   22      }
   23
   24      fn execute(app: &mut App, arg: Option<&str>) -> CommandResult {
   25          attach(app, arg)
   26      }
   27  }
   28
   29  fn attach(app: &mut App, arg: Option<&str>) -> CommandResult {
   30      let Some(raw_path) = arg.map(str::trim).filter(|value| !value.is_empty()) else {
   31          return CommandResult::error("Usage: /attach <image-or-video-path>");
   32      };
   33
   34      let path = resolve_attachment_path(raw_path, &app.workspace);
   35      let Ok(path) = path.canonicalize() else {
   36          return CommandResult::error(format!("Attachment not found: {}", path.display()));
   37      };
   38      if !path.is_file() {
   39          return CommandResult::error(format!("Attachment is not a file: {}", path.display()));
   40      }
   41
   42      let Some(kind) = media_kind(&path) else {
   43          return CommandResult::error(
   44              "Unsupported attachment type. /attach is for image/video paths; use @path for text files or directories.",
   45          );
   46      };
   47
   48      // Validate an image here, not only at send time. The extension check above
   49      // trusts the filename; this reads the bytes, so a mislabelled, oversized or
   50      // corrupt file is refused while the user is still looking at the command
   51      // that caused it — rather than becoming a notice buried in a turn they have
   52      // already sent.
   53      if kind == "image"
   54          && let Err(error) = crate::image_attach::attach_image_from_path(&path)
   55      {
   56          return CommandResult::error(error.to_string());
   57      }
   58
   59      app.insert_media_attachment(kind, &path, None);
   60      CommandResult::message(format!("Attached {kind}: {}", path.display()))
   61  }
   62
   63  fn resolve_attachment_path(raw_path: &str, workspace: &Path) -> PathBuf {
   64      let unquoted = raw_path.trim().trim_matches('"').trim_matches('\'');
   65      let path = expand_home(unquoted);
   66      if path.is_absolute() {
   67          path
   68      } else {
   69          workspace.join(path)
   70      }

    4  use crate::commands::traits::{CommandInfo, RegisterCommand};
    5  use crate::localization::{Locale, MessageId, tr};
    6  use crate::tui::app::{App, AppAction, AutomationAction};
    7
    8  pub(in crate::commands) const COMMAND_INFO: CommandInfo = CommandInfo {
    9      name: "automation",
   10      aliases: &["automations", "scheduled"],
   11      usage: "/automation [list|show <id>|pause <id>|resume <id>|delete <id> [--confirm <token>]|run <id>]",
   12      description_id: MessageId::CmdAutomationDescription,
   13  };
   14
   15  pub(in crate::commands) struct AutomationCmd;
   16
   17  impl RegisterCommand for AutomationCmd {
   18      fn info() -> &'static CommandInfo {
   19          &COMMAND_INFO
   20      }
   21
   22      fn execute(app: &mut App, arg: Option<&str>) -> CommandResult {
   23          automation(app.ui_locale, arg)
   24      }
   25  }
   26
   27  fn automation(locale: Locale, args: Option<&str>) -> CommandResult {
   28      let raw = args.unwrap_or("").trim();
   29      if raw.is_empty() || raw.eq_ignore_ascii_case("list") {
   30          return action(AutomationAction::List);
   31      }
   32
   33      let mut parts = raw.split_whitespace();
   34      let verb = parts.next().unwrap_or("").to_ascii_lowercase();
   35
   36      match verb.as_str() {
   37          "show" | "status" => single_id(locale, &mut parts, AutomationAction::Show),
   38          "pause" => single_id(locale, &mut parts, AutomationAction::Pause),
   39          "resume" => single_id(locale, &mut parts, AutomationAction::Resume),
   40          "delete" | "remove" | "rm" => delete(locale, &mut parts),
   41          "run" | "trigger" => single_id(locale, &mut parts, AutomationAction::Run),
   42          _ => usage_error(locale),
   43      }
   44  }
   45
   46  fn single_id<'a>(
   47      locale: Locale,
   48      parts: &mut impl Iterator<Item = &'a str>,
   49      make_action: fn(String) -> AutomationAction,
   50  ) -> CommandResult {
   51      let Some(id) = parts.next() else {
   52          return usage_error(locale);
   53      };
   54      if parts.next().is_some() {
   55          return usage_error(locale);
   56      }
   57      action(make_action(id.to_string()))
   58  }
   59
   60  fn delete<'a>(locale: Locale, parts: &mut impl Iterator<Item = &'a str>) -> CommandResult {
   61      let Some(id) = parts.next() else {
   62          return usage_error(locale);
   63      };
   64      let confirmation = match (parts.next(), parts.next(), parts.next()) {
   65          (None, None, None) => None,
   66          (Some(flag), Some(token), None) if flag.eq_ignore_ascii_case("--confirm") => {
   67              Some(token.to_string())
   68          }

/branch

interactive slash command · command module boundary · crates/tui/src/commands/groups/session/branch.rs:1

An interactive slash command; this card shows its registry entry and the boundary into its handler.

/branch→parse→route→policy→handler→receipt
Expand line-level source evidence
    1  use super::CommandResult;
    2  use crate::commands::traits::{CommandInfo, RegisterCommand};
    3  use crate::localization::MessageId;
    4  use crate::tui::app::App;
    5  pub(in crate::commands) const COMMAND_INFO: CommandInfo = CommandInfo {
    6      name: "branch",
    7      aliases: &[],
    8      usage: "/branch <entry_id>",
    9      description_id: MessageId::CmdBranchDescription,
   10  };
   11  pub(in crate::commands) struct BranchCmd;
   12  impl RegisterCommand for BranchCmd {
   13      fn info() -> &'static CommandInfo {
   14          &COMMAND_INFO
   15      }
   16      fn execute(app: &mut App, arg: Option<&str>) -> CommandResult {
   17          branch(app, arg)
   18      }
   19  }
   20  fn branch(app: &mut App, arg: Option<&str>) -> CommandResult {
   21      if app.session_transition_blocked() {
   22          return CommandResult::error(
   23              "Cannot branch while runtime work is active. Wait for the turn to finish, or cancel it first.",
   24          );
   25      }
   26      let Some(entry_id) = arg.map(str::trim).filter(|s| !s.is_empty()) else {
   27          if let Some(session_id) = app.current_session_id.as_deref()
   28              && let Ok(manager) = crate::session_manager::SessionManager::default_location()
   29              && let Ok(mut session) = manager.load_session(session_id)
   30          {
   31              session.ensure_journal();
   32              if let Some(journal) = session.journal.as_ref()
   33                  && let Some(leaf) = journal.leaf_id.as_deref()
   34              {
   35                  return CommandResult::message(format!(
   36                      "Current leaf: {leaf}\nUse `/branch <entry_id>` to move the leaf (history is never rewritten).\nUse `/tree` to list entry ids."
   37                  ));
   38              }
   39          }
   40          return CommandResult::message(
   41              "Usage: /branch <entry_id>\nMoves the active leaf to an existing entry. Future appends become children of that entry.\nHistory is never rewritten — branching only moves the leaf.\n\nUse `/tree` to see entry ids.",
   42          );
   43      };
   44      let session_id = match app.current_session_id.clone() {
   45          Some(id) => id,
   46          None => {
   47              return CommandResult::error(
   48                  "No active session to branch. Resume or create a session first.",
   49              );
   50          }
   51      };
   52      let manager = match crate::session_manager::SessionManager::default_location() {
   53          Ok(m) => m,
   54          Err(e) => return CommandResult::error(format!("could not open sessions directory: {e}")),
   55      };
   56      let mut session = match manager.load_session(&session_id) {
   57          Ok(s) => s,
   58          Err(e) => return CommandResult::error(format!("could not load session {session_id}: {e}")),
   59      };
   60      session.ensure_journal();
Open this command's dynamic execution map →

   35                  open_status(app);
   36                  CommandResult::ok()
   37              }
   38              Some("preview") => {
   39                  open_preview(app);
   40                  CommandResult::ok()
   41              }

   44                  CommandResult::ok()

   49              }
   50              Some("explain" | "agents") => {
   51                  open_explanation(app);
   52                  CommandResult::ok()
   53              }
   54              Some("edit" | "guided" | "custom") => {
   55                  CommandResult::action(AppAction::OpenSetupWizardAt {
   56                      step: SetupStep::Constitution,
   57                  })
   58              }
   59              Some("repair" | "fix") => CommandResult::with_message_and_action(
   60                  repair_text(app.ui_locale),
   61                  AppAction::OpenSetupWizardAt {
   62                      step: SetupStep::Constitution,
   63                  },
   64              ),
   65              Some("posture" | "runtime-posture") => {
   66                  CommandResult::action(AppAction::OpenSetupWizardAt {
   67                      step: SetupStep::TrustSandbox,
   68                  })
   69              }
   70              Some("bundled" | "default" | "use-bundled" | "use-default") => {
   71                  CommandResult::action(AppAction::UseBundledConstitution)
   72              }
   73              // The exact effective base prompt for the next turn (#3928). Routed
   74              // as an action because the assembly needs the session config, so the
   75              // preview is built by the same function the dispatch path uses
   76              // rather than a lookalike reconstruction.
   77              Some("base" | "prompt" | "base-prompt" | "effective") => {
   78                  CommandResult::action(AppAction::PreviewEffectiveBasePrompt)

interactive slash command · command module boundary · crates/tui/src/commands/groups/core/core.rs:16
Source ↗

/// Show help information

/core→parse→route→policy→handler→receipt
Expand line-level source evidence
   11  #[cfg(test)]
   12  use crate::tui::app::ReasoningEffort;
   13  use crate::tui::app::{App, AppAction, AppMode};
   14  use crate::tui::views::{HelpView, ModalKind, SubAgentsView, subagent_view_agents};
   15
   16  use super::CommandResult;
   17
   18  /// Show help information
   19  pub fn help(app: &mut App, topic: Option<&str>) -> CommandResult {
   20      if let Some(topic) = topic {
   21          let user_commands = crate::commands::user_registry::with_registry_for_workspace(
   22              Some(&app.workspace),
   23              Clone::clone,
   24          );
   25          if let Some(command) = user_commands.get(topic) {
   26              return CommandResult::message(user_command_help(app.ui_locale, command));
   27          }
   28
   29          // Show help for specific command
   30          if let Some(cmd) = crate::commands::get_command_info(topic) {
   31              let mut help = format!(
   32                  "{}\n\n  {}\n\n  {} {}",
   33                  cmd.name,
   34                  cmd.description_for(app.ui_locale),
   35                  tr(app.ui_locale, MessageId::HelpUsageLabel),
   36                  cmd.usage
   37              );
   38              let visible_aliases = cmd
   39                  .aliases
   40                  .iter()
   41                  .filter(|alias| user_commands.get(alias).is_none())
   42                  .copied()
   43                  .collect::<Vec<_>>();
   44              if !visible_aliases.is_empty() {
   45                  let _ = write!(
   46                      help,
   47                      "\n  {} {}",
   48                      tr(app.ui_locale, MessageId::HelpAliasesLabel),
   49                      visible_aliases.join(", ")
   50                  );
   51              }
   52              if cmd.name == "config" {
   53                  help.push_str(
   54                      "\n\n  Provider context window: set `context_window = 262144` under the active `[providers.<name>]` table to cap a 1M model to 256K. Use `/config context_window` to inspect the configured and effective values.",
   55                  );
   56              }
   57              return CommandResult::message(help);
   58          }
   59
   60          // Skills are user-invocable but were never a `/help` topic (#3912):
   61          // they execute and autocomplete, yet the surface that teaches the
   62          // product claimed they did not exist.
   63          if let Some(help) = skill_help(app, topic) {
   64              return CommandResult::message(help);
   65          }
   66
   67          return CommandResult::error(
   68              tr(app.ui_locale, MessageId::HelpUnknownCommand).replace("{topic}", topic),
   69          );
   70      }
   71
   72      // Show help overlay
   73      if app.view_stack.top_kind() != Some(ModalKind::Help) {
   74          let help = HelpView::new_for_workspace(app.ui_locale, &app.workspace, &app.cached_skills);
   75          app.view_stack.push(help);
Open this command's dynamic execution map →
